STIL (full available entry and applicable directory notes):

> COMMENT: The game chooses one of three tunes to play at random for the in-game
>          music, based on the value of the C64's timer, and chooses again at the
>          end of play to make the in-game soundtrack. Subtune #1 in this rip
>          replicates this so will play a random choice of the tunes. Subtunes #2
>          to #4 have been added to play each tune individually.
> (#1)
> COMMENT: Plays tune #2, #3 and #4 in random order.
> (#2)
>   TITLE: Blue Is The Colour
>  ARTIST: Daniel Boone &amp; Rod McQueen
> COMMENT: Performed by Chelsea FC for their 1972 League Cup final single, and
>          an anthem played at the club since then.
> (#3)
>   TITLE: Nice One Cyril
>  ARTIST: Harold Spiro &amp; Helen Clarke (The Cockerel Chorus)
> COMMENT: Made for Tottenham Hotspur FC for their 1973 League Cup final single,
>          and sung on the terrces by fans since then.
> (#4)
>   TITLE: I'm Forever Blowing Bubbles
>  ARTIST: John Kelette
> COMMENT: Adopted by West Ham United FC during the 1940s as a popular football
>          chant, and has been used by their fans since then. The club also
>          re-recorded the song for their 1975 FA Cup final single.
